&lt;project_context>
# 项目: distill_sparse_swiglu

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-34887
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 只允许使用 westd-34887 的 cuda:0 和 cuda:1，禁止使用其他 GPU（原因：项目分配 GPU 为 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell × 2，Reviewer 要求配置 GPU 边界以满足 L1 红线 #4） [来自 D001]


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：提交 lm_eval_kl_s42 到 westd-34887 cuda:0

### 目标
在 westd-34887 上用 cuda:0 运行 KL predictor (s42) 的 downstream benchmark eval，与 dense baseline 对比。

### 环境
- 服务器: westd-34887, account=root
- Python: /root/miniconda3/bin/python（conda base 环境）
- 激活: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 工作目录: /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/
- KL checkpoint: /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt
- GPU: cuda:0（已空闲）

### 命令
参考 lm_eval_dense 的命令格式：
```
python -u src/eval_benchmark.py --condition dense --tasks lm_eval --skip_ppl --gpu 0 --batch_size 4
```

对于 KL sparse condition，命令应该类似：
```
python -u src/eval_benchmark.py --condition kl_sparse --checkpoint checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt --tasks lm_eval --skip_ppl --gpu 0 --batch_size 4
```

### 步骤
1. SSH 到服务器，验证 checkpoint 文件存在
2. 检查 `src/eval_benchmark.py --help` 确认 --condition kl_sparse 参数名称正确
3. 用 `submit_training_job` 提交实验（exp_id=lm_eval_kl_s42, server=westd-34887, gpu_ids=[0], account=root, work_dir=/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/）
4. 提交命令前加环境激活: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python -u src/eval_benchmark.py ...`

### 注意
- GPU 编号从 0 开始
- 用 submit_training_job，不要用 nohup
- 提交后立即结束，不要轮询结果
- 如果 eval_benchmark.py 没有 --condition kl_sparse 参数，查看它支持什么参数名，适配正确的参数

### Provenance spec 补充字段
- data_path: "lm-eval cached datasets"
- data_count: 0
- model_name: "llama-3.1-8b"
- seed: 42
- checkpoint_source: "mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42"
- eval_data: "arc_challenge, winogrande, mmlu, gsm8k"

STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", BCE treats each neuron as an independent binary classification, losing inter-neuron coordination and output distribution signal. The predictor barely learned meaningful patterns (BCE only 5% better than random). KL's direct output-distribution matching provides much stronger learning signal. {\\\"linearity_ratio\\\": 0.2644, \\\"linearity_ratio_std\\\": 0.0073, \\\"magnitude_ratio\\\": 0.1578, \\\"magnitude_ratio_std\\\": 0.0088, \\\"cosine_similarity\\\": 0.1962, \\\"conclusion_type\\\": \\\"weak_subsumption\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\\\n\\\\n### 核心指标\\\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\\\n\\\\n### Effect magnitudes\\\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\\\n\\\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\\\n\\\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\\\\\"marginal complement\\\\\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\\\\\"subsumption\\\\\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\\\n\\\\n### 产出文件\\\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\\\"}, \\\"bce_isocompute_s42_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"ste_baseline_s42\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T18:35:42.177Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T20:03:55.571Z\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"STE baseline 代码实现完成 (Worker worker_code_ste_baseline_20260515T183635)。修改：src/predictor.py 新增 STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\\\n\\\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\\\n\\\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\\\n\\\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\\\n\\\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\\\n\\\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\\\n\\\\n## 根因分析\\\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\\\n\\\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"STE relaxation (hard threshold + straight-through gradient) 是否能匹配 Gumbel-sigmoid 的 KL distillation 效果？如果差距大，说明 Gumbel soft mask 对 KL 训练至关重要（Reviewer Concern #3 defense）。\\\", 阈值)\\\\n消除 seq_len mismatch 后 SPON recovery 从 3.8% 提升到 7.3%，说明 mismatch 确实是部分原因，但即使在匹配条件下 static bias 仍只能恢复不到 10% 的稀疏化误差。确认 D009 \\\\\\\"KL subsumes SPON\\\\\\\" reframe：KL predictor 的 per-token mask 已经很好地适应了重要 neuron，残余误差不是简单的 additive bias 能补偿的。\\\"}, \\\"eval_spon_supp_retry\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"PREPARING\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T19:44:22.659Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T19:40:29.742Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"BCE 在等量 FLOPs (2700 步 vs KL 1000 步, ratio=2.71x) 下是否能缩小与 KL 的 PPL 差距？如果不能，说明 KL 的优势来自 loss 函数本身而非计算量（Reviewer Concern #2 defense）。\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Iso-compute BCE: 2700 步匹配 KL 1000 步的总 FLOPs (ratio=2.71x, 实测 profile_flops.py: KL 0.549s/step vs BCE 0.202s/step @BS=1,SL=512)。\\\\\\\\nReviewer Concern #2 defense: 验证 KL 优势来自 loss 函数本身而非计算量差异。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## FLOPs 计算依据\\\\\\\\n- KL 每步: dense fwd (no grad) + sparse fwd (grad) + backward w/ gradient checkpointing = 0.549s\\\\\\\\n- BCE 每步: dense fwd (no grad) + predictor fwd/bwd (~0.9% model FLOPs) = 0.202s\\\\\\\\n- Ratio: 2.71x → 1000 KL steps ≈ 2700 BCE steps\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 失败记录 [2026-05-15]\\\\\\\\n进程在 Step 500/2700 后静默退出（PID 120879）。日志仅 17 行，最后记录 Step 500: corrected to 8.98 with --sparsity_target 0.3 (model_level_sparsity=0.300)\", \"model_level_sparsity\": 0.3, \"eval_dataset\": \"wikitext-2\", \"eval_seq_len\": 2048}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"D016 决策：sparsity=0.285（target=0.30），lambda_max=5000 仍无法推到 target。Director 接受为实际数据点——predictor 在 ~28.5% 有自然下限，不再重跑。论文报 achieved sparsity 28.5%（不写 target 30%）。\\n\\n## PPL 评估修正 (D018)\\n原 teal_sparse_ppl=8.76 使用默认 sparsity_target=0.5 评估，无效。\\n修正后 --sparsity_target 0.3: teal_sparse_ppl=8.98, model_level_sparsity=0.300。\\nPer-layer sparsity: 早期层 0.13-0.23, 中间层 0.30-0.34, 后层 0.50-0.70。\"}, \"lm_eval_mistral_downstream\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:34:22.527Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:34:22.528Z\"}, \"kl_sparsity70_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:34:18.823Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:27:38.526Z\", \"metrics\": {\"true_dense_ppl\": 6.12, \"predictor_hard_mask_ppl\": 13.84, \"teal_sparse_ppl\": 17.91, \"teal_sparse_ppl_static_baseline\": 17.91, \"model_level_sparsity\": 0.7, \"teal_sparse_note\": \"predictor TEAL eval @ sparsity_target=0.7; static baseline TEAL=17.91 保留在 teal_sparse_ppl_static_baseline\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Training Analysis (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Training Summary\\n- 1000/1000 steps completed, EXIT_CODE=0\\n- Sparsity converged to ~0.688 (target 0.70, within 0.65-0.75 success range)\\n- Lambda hit cap 1000 by step 10, tau annealed 0.999 → 0.100\\n- KL loss: 1.18 → 0.61\\n\\n### PPL Evaluation Results\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: **13.84** (per-token adaptive mask)\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: **17.91** (static global topk, FIXED via D017)\\n- model_level_sparsity: 0.699\\n\\n### D017 Bug Fix (2026-05-15)\\nOriginal teal_sparse_ppl=136,069 was caused by using raw sigmoid scores for global topk mask selection. Sigmoid probabilities lack magnitude information and are poor proxies for static neuron importance. Fix: score neurons by mean(|intermediate| * predictor_hard_mask) — activation magnitude weighted by predictor decisions. Also added per-layer mean normalization (from _global_topk_masks). Result: 136K → 17.91.\"}, \"mistral_kl_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:12:33.050Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:17:54.235Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Mistral-7B-v0.3 跨架构验证：KL normalized loss @ 50% sparsity。验证 KL distillation 方法在非 Llama 架构上也有效。Plan_009 第一个实验。\", \"metrics\": {\"train_final_loss\": 0.2188, \"train_final_kl_raw\": 0.2188, \"train_final_kl_norm\": 0.2188, \"train_final_constraint\": 0, \"train_final_sparsity\": 0.48, \"train_final_lambda\": 1000, \"train_final_tau\": 0.1, \"train_steps_completed\": 1000, \"true_dense_ppl\": 5.52, \"predictor_hard_mask_ppl\": 7.3, \"teal_sparse_ppl\": 8.98, \"model_level_sparsity\": 0.5, \"eval_dataset\": \"wikitext-2\", \"eval_seq_len\": 2048, \"calibration_samples\": 32, \"eval_samples\": 159}, \"notes\": \"## Mistral-7B-v0.3 KL distillation @ 50% sparsity (plan_009)\\n\\n### 训练 (1000 steps, EXIT_CODE=0)\\n- Final loss: 0.2188, sparsity: 0.480, lambda: 1000, tau: 0.100\\n\\n### WikiText-2 PPL (50% sparsity)\\n| Metric | Mistral-7B | Llama-3.1-8B |\\n|--------|-----------|--------------|\\n| true_dense_ppl | **5.52** | 6.12 |\\n| predictor_hard_mask_ppl | **7.30** (1.32x) | 9.19±0.04 (5-seed) |\\n| teal_sparse_ppl | **8.98** (1.63x) | 12.32±0.71 (5-seed mean, incl s456 TEAL outlier); 12.00±0.07 (4-seed excl s456) |\\n| model_level_sparsity | 0.500 | 0.498 |\\n\\n### 结论: SUCCESS (cross-architecture generalization)\\nPPL 8.98 远低于 15 阈值。Mistral 降级比例更小 (1.63x vs Llama 1.96x 4-seed)。\\nPer-layer sparsity 分布合理：前后层高 (~0.6)，中间层低 (~0.36-0.47)。\\n⚠️ 仅单 seed，Reviewer 可能要求补 seed。Downstream eval (ARC-c, MMLU) 待跑。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"lm_eval_kl_s42\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:34:42.788Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T23:03:34.023Z\"}, \"training_free_baselines\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T18:31:40.470Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:55:30.308Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": {\"dense_ppl\": 6.12, \"uniform_50pct_ppl\": 23.9, \"teal_greedy_ppl\": 33.47, \"vanilla_teal_global_ppl\": 23.31, \"wina_global_ppl\": 27.93, \"rsparse_global_ppl\": 151.2}, \"notes\": \"## 2026-05-16 Re-evaluation (CRITICAL bug fix)\\n\\n### Bug: _global_topk_masks 缺少 per-layer mean normalization\\n激活幅度跨层差异 50-100x，导致 global topk 完全剪枝 early layers、完全保留 late layers。修复：对每层 importance score 先除以层均值再做 global topk。\\n\\n### 修正后结果 (50% sparsity, LLaMA 3.1 8B, WikiText-2)\\n| Method | Before (bug) | After (fixed) |\\n|--------|-------------|--------------|\\n| Vanilla TEAL global | 58,032 | **23.31** |\\n| WINA global | 82,567 | **27.93** |\\n| TEAL Greedy | 50,035 | **33.47** |\\n| R-Sparse | 74,929 | 151.20 (partial fix) |\\n\\n3 个可用 training-free baselines: Vanilla TEAL, WINA, TEAL Greedy。\\nR-Sparse 需要 percentile/max normalization 才能正常工作（SVD scores 分布不同）。\"}, \"lm_eval_dense_test\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:30:54.405Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T22:30:54.405Z\"}, \"kl_sparsity30_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:30:30.528Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T21:14:59.706Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"KL distillation @ 30% sparsity (mild pruning). W&B run was offline (not synced).\"}}, \"bce_isocompute_v2_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:01:43.104Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T21:11:42.245Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"BCE 在 iso-compute (1000 steps) + iso-sparsity (STE reg_weight=2.0 使 sparsity 收敛到 ~0.50) 下是否能缩小与 KL 的 PPL 差距？消除 compute 和 sparsity 两个 confound。\", \"notes\": \"## Eval Results (2026-05-16)\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: 26.76\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: 54.84\\n- sparsity_target: 0.50 (converged correctly, STE fix worked)\\n\\n## Training Summary\\n- 1000 steps, 1048s (1.05s/step), EXIT_CODE=0\\n- BCE loss: 0.6714 → 0.6586 (only 5% better than random 0.693)\\n- Reg loss: 0.0171 → 0.0051 (sparsity converged to 0.50)\\n- reg_weight=2.0, Gumbel-sigmoid STE\\n\\n## Comparison with KL Baseline\\n- KL normalized (50% sparsity, 4-seed mean): teal_sparse_ppl = 12.00 ± 0.07\\n- BCE v2: teal_sparse_ppl = 54.84 — 4.6x worse than KL\\n\\n## Conclusion\\nBCE + STE dramatically underperforms KL at 50% sparsity under iso-compute (1000 steps). BCE treats each neuron as an independent binary classification, losing inter-neuron coordination and output distribution signal. The predictor barely learned meaningful patterns (BCE only 5% better than random). KL's direct output-distribution matching provides much stronger learning signal. BCE is not a viable alternative loss for sparsity predictor training.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": {\"final_loss\": 0.6637, \"final_bce_loss\": 0.6586, \"final_reg_loss\": 0.0051, 482.65, \"spon_correction_norm\": 76.2, \"interaction_norm\": 147.74, \"num_samples\": 50, \"seq_len\": 2048, \"conclusion_type\": \"weak_subsumption\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\"}, \"bce_isocompute_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\",
